# Spec / test coverage audit ledger

- Status: static audit record plus authorized follow-up. Assertion findings are source evidence, not execution pass claims. B79-04 is closed as a 127-condition evidence-mapping/reconciliation deliverable; that does not assert behavioral proof for every condition. Latest scoped checks and remaining gaps are recorded below and in the backlog.
- Baseline HEAD: `2dca907cf2b8b8dbc1aa873dde6e60b753a61355` (2026-10-18), from the Plan79 task context.
- Scope: all 35 scenarios and API contracts in [`spec.md`](../../openspec/specs/stcirt/spec.md), plus every active, out-of-scenario, and retired CSV row.
- Inputs: [`spec-test-traceability.md`](spec-test-traceability.md) and Plan78 ledgers/runtime manifest were read-only. The two `R1-S3` scenario links for `VIT-0111` and `VIT-0113` were added during B79-10 resolution. Supplemental B79-04 mapping repair links `VIT-0380` to only the R3-S2 legacy-persistence subclause and adds `R5-S4` to `E2E78-06`; neither expands the test guarantee beyond its cited assertion boundary.
- Roster basis: CSV contains 1,192 active (1,108 Vitest, 31 Playwright declarations, 53 benchmarks) and 33 retired. Current Plan78 Vitest inventory exactly matches all active IDs/path/titles; historical 1,111 was current 1,108 plus retired VIT-0506–0508. Plan78 recorded 30 Playwright declarations × 2 configured projects = 60 runtime instances; after adding `E2E78-32`, the current roster has 31 declarations and 62 configured project instances. Benchmarks are counted separately.
- Audit method: source clauses and current source-derived case roster were cross-checked statically. Exact title/path/ID match is established for Vitest against the Plan78 manifest; Playwright and benchmark cases use their Plan78 source inventories. Assertions are static source locations, not execution results. Mock/SQLite boundaries are called out. Every Plan79 condition has assertion evidence or an explicit condition-level gap; remaining gaps and unrun checks are summarized in the backlog.

Roster delta: Vitest has no CSV-only/source-only IDs and no ID/path/title mismatches versus the current manifest; historical extra VIT-0506–0508 are retired and absent from current inventory. Plan78 Playwright 30 source declarations executed in two projects (60 instances); current CSV stores 31 declarations (62 configured instances), including E2E78-32. Benchmarks are 53 expanded registrations, not acceptance tests. The roster reconciliation itself was static; the separately reported targeted runtime verification is listed below.

## Verification status

Latest reported targeted checks (2026-10-18): Vitest, 9 files / 371 tests passed; targeted Chromium E2E, 3 invocations / 3 passed: `E2E78-19` all rank entries fit on the slide under reduced motion and `E2E78-20` rank winner reveal plays once per stage entry and not on same-stage refresh, 2 passed (19.8s); `E2E78-32` nested interactive controls keyboard suppression, 1 passed (8.1s). `pnpm exec oxfmt --check tests/e2e/presentation.spec.ts` passed; `pnpm exec oxlint tests/e2e/presentation.spec.ts` exited 0 with a warning at pre-existing line 2923; `git diff --check` passed. An ID-only grep returning no-tests-found did not execute tests and is not a failed test run. Historical focused runs: 7 files / 360 tests; deck repository + public-route tests, 2 files / 111 tests; participant-results route-to-test-SQLite integration, 1 file / 4 tests. These earlier runs remain provenance for their scoped checks, not the latest overall result. No full suite, full lint/build, live provider, production database, or deployed scheduler result is implied. The lint warning remains visible and was not introduced by this audit.

## Scenario coverage navigation

The former 35-row narrative summary is removed because the atomic condition matrix below is the authoritative scenario inventory. Use its stable `R*-S*-Axx` IDs for condition-level findings; the active case crosswalk and OOS/retired appendices are the authoritative roster views.

## Atomic scenario condition matrix

Each condition below records one explicit WHEN→THEN pair from a Scenario block with exact spec line references. All 35 scenario IDs appear. Alternative values explicitly grouped by the spec remain grouped; no additional expected behavior is introduced. Each condition links CSV cases whose `spec_scenario_ids` name that scenario and gives static assertion locations or a visible evidence gap. The audit overlay table preserves the history and verification of the repaired `R1-S3` CSV mappings. A case name or historical pass is not execution proof. This matrix scopes Scenario acceptance to explicit WHEN→THEN blocks. API contract clauses are separately itemized in the API inventory. Plan79 Phase 1 (`shared_plan/79-SPEC_TEST_COVERAGE_AUDIT_PLAN.md`, lines 51–56) requires those 35 Scenario conditions and route-level API contract clauses; its closeout criteria at lines 125–126 do not require an independent ID for every other descriptive Requirements sentence.

## Static redundancy review (B79-09)

Static exact-title review identified `VIT-0231` and `VIT-0232` as the only same-parameterized-test pair in the review. Their inputs differ: missing bearer and wrong bearer exercise distinct authorization failure cases. Neither is a deletion candidate under Plan79's same-condition, same-failure-mode, and same-layer criteria. The additional E2E78-32 title was checked and creates no duplicate candidate. No case was removed. This bounded static review does not claim exhaustive nonredundancy across all 1,192 active cases.

## API method/route condition inventory

Every API entry is split by HTTP method and route. Each semicolon-separated normative clause is a distinct condition row with its own stable ID, expected contract, assertion source, candidate route CSV IDs, and finding. CSV IDs are listed in the active case crosswalk except documented audit overlays and mapping discrepancies; the OOS/retired appendices carry every row. Route-test repository mocks prove HTTP boundary behavior only; repository tests use transaction test SQLite, not live Turso. The inventory and matrix reconciliation are static source reviews. Targeted test executions are recorded in “Verification status”; those results do not establish unrun checks or live service behavior.

## Condition-level treatment for remaining partial findings (B79-04)

The matrix above remains authoritative for each condition's existing assertion evidence and observed value. The following register makes the unsupported conjunct, searched source, next action, and accountable role explicit for every remaining partial/gap condition in the B79-04 scope. A listed gap is an evidence gap, not a claim that implementation behavior is defective. “Unassigned” means the repository does not identify an accountable person or operational role; the next action includes assigning one before claiming closure. No condition is promoted to direct by this register.

The original condition register above has 56 distinct IDs. Final reconciliation of the delegated slices found 61 additional partial conditions and 10 further API rows (`API-PS-POST-A01`, `API-QB-A04/A05`, and `API-RS-GET-A01–A07`) that were outside the earlier exclusion list. The following 71-row addendum records each once; together the B79-04 treatment register now contains 127 distinct IDs (56 + 71). The ID set was obtained by scanning every Scenario/API matrix row whose status contains Partial/Gap, extracting its leading condition ID, excluding IDs already present in the 56-row register, and checking the resulting IDs against the additional-row auditor report. Closed backlog items are not listed as open candidates. These entries preserve the existing matrix evidence and do not promote any condition to direct.

## Open evidence and condition gaps

- **Requirement prose coverage:** the explicit 35 Scenario WHEN/THEN blocks are represented by 78 atomic condition rows after failure-mode splitting of R1-S1; method/route API contracts are separately itemized. Plan79 requires these atoms and contracts to have assertion evidence or an explicit gap, not a separate ID for every descriptive sentence. Static inventory completion does not mean every condition is behaviorally verified.
- **Open backlog IDs:** B79-04 is closed as a completed 127-condition evidence-map and CSV reconciliation; B79-08 remains intentionally open for human assistive-technology/device/200% physical-zoom checks; B79-12 remains intentionally open for JEV operations ownership, production scheduler/secret/monitoring, and provider environment. B79-02 is closed for source-line extraction and browser evidence mapping; its human/external checks are tracked under B79-08. B79-03/05/06/07/09/10/11 are closed and are not unresolved candidates.
- **B79-04 reconciliation:** 127 unique Partial/Gap condition IDs have clause-level gap/action/owner entries (56 original + 71 newly reconciled). Cross-slice scan found 61 rows in R2/R3/R6/R7/R8/API-AL/API-AP/API-DECK plus 10 omitted API rows (`API-PS-POST-A01`, `API-QB-A04/A05`, `API-RS-GET-A01–A07`). R9 route-to-test-SQLite evidence is now recorded in its Scenario rows; this is not live Turso evidence. No condition was promoted to direct by the register.
- **CSV mapping repairs:** B79-10 rows `VIT-0111` and `VIT-0113` retain their original links plus R1-S3. Supplemental B79-04 repairs link `VIT-0380` only to the R3-S2 legacy-persistence subclause while retaining its OOS primary category, add R5-S4 alongside R5-S2 for `E2E78-06`, and register the new `E2E78-32` declaration for R8-S5. Active/retired totals are 1,192/33; the 194 OOS category rows overlap the VIT-0380 partial cross-link.
- **Conditional Deck check:** `API-DECK-A01` has local SQLite revision/source fallback and transaction evidence plus mocked route coverage. A concurrent source-row mutation race remains only if that race is a required contract; it is not presented as an established failure.
- **External conditions:** actual AT/device/zoom validation and live JEV scheduler/provider operation are not established by component, route-mock, adapter-mock or test-SQLite evidence. The scheduler still needs an operations owner, deployed invocation, matching secret/configuration, and monitoring evidence.
- **Execution status:** successful targeted Vitest runs are recorded under Verification status and the backlog. They do not establish unrun checks, production/live database behavior, browser-computed behavior beyond the cited E2E cases, or external service operation. No outcome is inferred from static registration or historic pass records.
- **Current remaining work:** B79-08 intentionally retains human checks of actual assistive-technology announcements/device behavior and 200% physical zoom; B79-12 intentionally retains external JEV operations ownership, deployed scheduler/secret alignment, monitoring, and provider-environment verification. B79-02 is closed: source-line extraction and existing browser-computed opacity/timing evidence are mapped, while human/external checks remain explicit under B79-08/B79-12. B79-04's 127 unique treatments close the mapping/reconciliation task only; conditions with partial assertions or gaps remain partial/gap as written in the matrix.
